- `_selectedDay` = `data.Day`
- `_selectedDay` = `day`
- `_selectedDay` = `target`
- `b.Modulate` = `day < data.Day ? new Color("8591a0") : Colors.White`
- `b.Pressed` += `() => { _selectedDay = day; Render(); }`
- `Bond` = `ordinaryDate.Conversation == null ? null : RelationshipPresentation.IconBond(PrivateMessages.Mailbox(data).Relations.GetValueOrDefault(ordinaryDate.Conversation.PersonId) ?? new())`
- `Columns` = `7`
- `data.AutoQualifiers` = `!data.AutoQualifiers`
- `Description` = `match?.Event ?? appointment.Offer?.Title ?? world?.Name ?? "训练 / 新闻"`
- `game.Registered` = `false`
- `HasMatch` = `match != null || world != null`
- `Name` = `"CalendarDay_" + day`
- `nextMatchButton.Disabled` = `true`
- `nextMatchButton.TooltipText` = `"暂无已报名比赛或已确认活动。"`
- `Number` = `$"{GameCalendar.Date(data, day):M/d}"`
- `Question` = `data.Life.RandomEvents.Any(e => e.Day == day && e.State != "skipped")`
- `Registered` = `entered`
- `Selected` = `day == shown`
- `SizeFlagsHorizontal` = `SizeFlags.ExpandFill`
- `Today` = `day == data.Day`
- `Wedding` = `appointments[day].Any(a => a.Offer.GoodId == "grand-wedding")`

- `NationalSpire.CareerScreen.TeamButton(string, System.Action, int)`；输入表达式：`TeamButton("模拟本场", () =>              {                  if (MultiplayerCommand("propose-simulate", match.Id, number: (int)asc.Value)) return;                  if (chars.Count == 0) return;                  var character = selectedCharacter == chars.Count ? GameBridge.RandomChoice() : chars[Math.Clamp(selectedCharacter, 0, chars.Count - 1)];                  var error = PlayerSimulation.Run(data, match, character.Title.GetFormattedText(), character.Id.ToString(), (int)asc.Value);                  if (error == null && data.PendingSettlementId == match.Id && data.Results.Any(r => r.MatchId == match.Id)) _tab = "结算";                  Render(); if (error != null) Notice(error, true);              }, 160)`

- `NationalSpire.CareerScreen.TeamButton(string, System.Action, int)`；输入表达式：`TeamButton(due ? "开始比赛" : "跳转到比赛日", async () =>              {                  if (!due) { if (MultiplayerCommand("propose-advance", "direct", number: CareerEngine.NextMatch(data)?.Day ?? data.Day + 1)) return; CareerEngine.AdvanceToMatch(data); Render(); return; }                  if (chars.Count == 0) { Notice("没有可用角色。", true); return; }                  if (MultiplayerCommand("propose-start", match.Id, number: (int)asc.Value)) return;                  if (_starting) return;                  _starting = true;                  var character = selectedCharacter == chars.Count ? GameBridge.RandomChoice() : chars[Math.Clamp(selectedCharacter, 0, chars.Count - 1)];                  var error = await GameBridge.StartMatch(match, character, (int)asc.Value);                  _starting = false;                  if (IsInstanceValid(this) && !IsQueuedForDeletion() && error != null) Notice(error, true);              }, 220)`；返回值紧邻使用处：`EqualsValueClauseSyntax: = TeamButton(due ? "开始比赛" : "跳转到比赛日", async () =>              {                  if (!due) { if (MultiplayerCommand("propose-advance", "direct", number: CareerEngine.NextMatch(data)?.Day ?? data.Day + 1)) return; CareerEngine.AdvanceToMatch(data); Render(); return; }                  if (chars.Count == 0) { Notice("没有可用角色。", true); return; }                  if (MultiplayerCommand("propose-start", match.Id, number: (int)asc.Value)) return;                  if (_starting) return;                  _starting = true;                  var character = selectedCharacter == chars.Count ? GameBridge.RandomChoice() : chars[Math.Clamp(selectedCharacter, 0, chars.Count - 1)];                  var error = await GameBridge.StartMatch(match, character, (int)asc.Value);                  _starting = false;                  if (IsInstanceValid(this) && !IsQueuedForDeletion() && error != null) Notice(error, true);              }, 220)`
